- C-007 · **CLAIM** The templates cover all places in the pipeline where scenario-specific logic is needed: Step01 (sender adapter), Step03 (inbound conversion), Step04/05 (custom determination), Step07 (mapping + connection to outbound) and custom exits / error handling. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION 1.1.1 · WHERE templates.md:L11

- C-011 · **CLAIM** With RetryViaParentFlow the error is propagated to the calling generic step, which owns the retry; the template itself never retries. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION 1.1.1 · WHERE templates.md:L15

- C-015 · **CLAIM** Each template copy must have a unique ProcessDirect endpoint. · KIND procedure · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION 1.1.1 · WHERE templates.md:L19

## when-to-use-cloud-integration-pipeline-decision-matrix.md

- C-128 · **CLAIM** Pipeline v2 is the default approach for integration between systems in our environment. [tenant-specific] · KIND procedure · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION v2 · WHERE when-to-use-cloud-integration-pipeline-decision-matrix.md:L10
- C-129 · **CLAIM** Pipeline v2 is meant for asynchronous processing (A2A/B2B, IDoc/XI/proxy, master data distribution). · KIND rationale · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION v2 · WHERE when-to-use-cloud-integration-pipeline-decision-matrix.md:L10
- C-130 · **CLAIM** For synchronous request/reply scenarios (e.g. an ABAP proxy as receiver) the pipeline is an optional approach. · KIND procedure · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION v2 · WHERE when-to-use-cloud-integration-pipeline-decision-matrix.md:L11
- C-131 · **CLAIM** A clearly atypical one-off, where the generic pattern adds only overhead, gets a single iflow. · KIND procedure · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE when-to-use-cloud-integration-pipeline-decision-matrix.md:L15

## pipeline-concept.md

- C-316 · **CLAIM** SAP recommends using the integrated messaging runtime. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L9
- C-317 · **CLAIM** Passages marked [FULLY DECOUPLED PIPELINE ONLY] are not used in the integrated runtime and are described for completeness and context. · KIND procedure · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L9
- C-318 · **CLAIM** The pipeline concept transfers the way messages are processed in SAP PI/PO to Cloud Integration. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L15
- C-319 · **CLAIM** Instead of every scenario having its own freely modelled iflow, a message passes through a fixed sequence of steps, always the same. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L15
- C-320 · **CLAIM** The fixed sequence of pipeline steps is receiver determination → interface determination → mapping → delivery. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L17
- C-321 · **CLAIM** The name "pipeline" comes from the message flowing through one pipe: a series of steps through which every message passes in the same order. · KIND rationale · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L19
- C-322 · **CLAIM** The logic that differs between scenarios (where to send the message, how to map it) is not hard-wired in each flow; it is configuration that the common generic steps load at runtime according to the scenario. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L19
- C-323 · **CLAIM** The pipeline concept originated for migrations from SAP PO, but today it makes sense for new scenarios as well. · KIND rationale · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L21
- C-324 · **CLAIM** Generic flows are the common pipeline steps, deployed once for all scenarios and maintained and updated by SAP. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L27
- C-325 · **CLAIM** Scenario-specific flows are copies of templates carrying the conversions, mappings and adapters of a particular scenario. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L28
- C-326 · **CLAIM** JMS queues decouple the steps, which gives persistence, restartability and guaranteed delivery. · KIND rationale · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L29
- C-327 · **CLAIM** ProcessDirect is used for synchronous calls between flows (generic step → scenario-specific flow). · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L30
- C-328 · **CLAIM** The Partner Directory (PD) holds the per-scenario configuration (behaviour and XSLT mappings), which is why one generic flow serves all scenarios. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L31
- C-329 · **CLAIM** With a 1:1 migration every async scenario would need its own JMS queue (easily thousands), whereas the tenant has hard limits; the pipeline gets by with a few queues shared by all scenarios. · KIND rationale · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L35
- C-330 · **CLAIM** The fully decoupled pipeline uses 4 queues (PIPQ01–PIPQ04) plus 4 DLQ, 8 in total. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L36
- C-331 · **CLAIM** The integrated messaging runtime uses 2 queues (PIPX01, PIPQ04) plus 2 DLQ, 4 in total. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L37
- C-332 · **CLAIM** The pipeline gives a ready-made operating model: retry, DLQ, deduplication on input and output, restart and uniform custom statuses need not be written or maintained. · KIND rationale · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L38
- C-333 · **CLAIM** Custom statuses are uniform names of message states in the monitor, by which it is possible to filter. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L38
- C-334 · **CLAIM** A message can be found in monitoring by a business key (document number, partner, message type) just by setting a property with the prefix customHeader_, without custom code. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L39
- C-335 · **CLAIM** A new partner of the same message type needs only a few records in the Partner Directory and no deployment. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L40
- C-336 · **CLAIM** A new message type needs a new scenario-specific flow (a copy of the template with the mapping and adapter), but the generic line does not change. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L40
- C-337 · **CLAIM** Only the small scenario-specific part is deployed and transported, not the whole infrastructure. · KIND rationale · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L40
- C-338 · **CLAIM** The Step07 templates (P2P, One-to-Many, Maintain Order, Sync, P2P→Generic XI/IDoc) have built-in standards: Exception Handling with the status RetryViaParentFlow, idempotent delivery and a custom post-processing exit. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L41
- C-339 · **CLAIM** Because the standards are built into the Step07 templates, the developer cannot omit them by mistake and only fills in the mapping, conversion and adapter. · KIND rationale · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L41
- C-340 · **CLAIM** All Step07 template variants share the same common frame and differ only in the delivery pattern. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L41
- C-341 · **CLAIM** With the standards built into the Step07 templates, code review is narrowed to the scenario-specific part. · KIND rationale · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L41
- C-342 · **CLAIM** The generic flows receive fixes and new functions from SAP (stage rules, bulk handling, DLQ behaviour…). · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L42
- C-343 · **CLAIM** The concept started with the fully decoupled pipeline, where every step is a separate iflow. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L46
- C-344 · **CLAIM** The fully decoupled pipeline is the most illustrative variant for understanding, because it is visible what happens where. · KIND rationale · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L46
- C-345 · **CLAIM** The integrated messaging runtime came into being because practice showed that so many separate steps (and thus queues and logs) are not needed; it handles the work of several steps in one. · KIND rationale · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L46
- C-346 · **CLAIM** The sync variant works without queues. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L46
- C-347 · **CLAIM** The fully decoupled pipeline has 7 steps (Step01 → … → Step07), integrated messaging async has 5, integrated messaging sync has 3. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L50
- C-348 · **CLAIM** JMS queues per variant: fully decoupled PIPQ01–Q04; integrated messaging async PIPX01 + PIPQ04; integrated messaging sync none. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L51
- C-349 · **CLAIM** In the fully decoupled pipeline receiver and interface determination are separate XSLTs (a combined one is possible too). · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L52
- C-350 · **CLAIM** In integrated messaging async receiver and interface determination is a combined XSLT only. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L52
- C-351 · **CLAIM** In integrated messaging sync receiver and interface determination is a combined XSLT with strictly 1 receiver and 1 interface. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L52
- C-352 · **CLAIM** Reuse of XRD from PO: yes in the fully decoupled pipeline; in integrated async only via custom RD, which must also return interfaces; no in integrated sync. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L53
- C-353 · **CLAIM** skipRcvDet is supported in the fully decoupled pipeline and not in integrated async or sync. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L54
- C-354 · **CLAIM** Custom interface determination is supported in the fully decoupled pipeline and not in integrated async or sync. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L55
- C-355 · **CLAIM** P2P bypass, custom error handling and receiver-specific queues are supported in the fully decoupled pipeline and in integrated async. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L56
- C-356 · **CLAIM** For integrated sync the comparison table states only "P2P yes" for the row P2P bypass / custom error handling / receiver-specific queues. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L56
- C-357 · **CLAIM** Whether integrated sync supports custom error handling and receiver-specific queues is not stated (the table cell reads only "P2P yes"). · KIND open-question · CONFIDENCE low · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L56
- C-358 · **CLAIM** The "no" for skipRcvDet and custom interface determination in the integrated runtime is not a loss of functionality. · KIND rationale · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L58
- C-359 · **CLAIM** Reuse XRD, skipRcvDet and custom interface determination are functions not used in the integrated runtime. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L58
- C-360 · **CLAIM** The seven-step fully decoupled variant is not used by us; the integrated async/sync applies. [tenant-specific] · KIND procedure · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L62
- C-361 · **CLAIM** In the integrated runtime the steps Step02/Step04/Step05 and the queues PIPQ01–PIPQ03 are merged. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L62
- C-362 · **CLAIM** Step01 – Inbound Processing (scenario-specific) holds the sender adapter. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L64
- C-363 · **CLAIM** Step01 sets the headers SAP_Sender and SAP_SenderInterface. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L64
- C-364 · **CLAIM** The pair SAP_Sender and SAP_SenderInterface uniquely determines the scenario. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L64
- C-365 · **CLAIM** In the fully decoupled pipeline Step01 writes the message to the input queue PIPQ01 as early as possible. · KIND fact · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L64
- C-366 · **CLAIM** Step01 writes the message to the queue as early as possible so that it is persistent and decoupled from the sender. · KIND rationale · CONFIDENCE medium · VERSION unknown · WHERE pipeline-concept.md:L64

## Name index

Each technical name of the claims above once (iFlows, scripts and their functions, headers, exchange properties, JMS queues, PD keys, externalized parameters, custom statuses), in case-insensitive order, with the C-ids that mention it (at most 12, then "…+n" further ids). A key suffix written `_<...>` in a claim is listed as `_*`.

- `addCalledByPipelineStep`: C-105, C-106
- `addCustomHeaderProperties`: C-111, C-112, C-113, C-167, C-588, C-589, C-684, C-685, C-708, C-712, C-717, C-820, …+56
- `addEdidcNodeIfMissing`: C-1816, C-1833, C-1859, C-1860, C-1861
- `addEntry`: C-123, C-851, C-1030, C-1465, C-1705
- `addPartyForXIInbound`: C-679, C-708, C-710, C-732, C-733, C-734, C-735
- `AllowedHeadersList`: C-600, C-607, C-1714, C-1761, C-1799
- `AlternativePartner`: C-452, C-453, C-454, C-483, C-557, C-560, C-622, C-988
- `AltPartner`: C-479
- `applyRoutingResult`: C-1275, C-1276, C-1277, C-1278, C-1439, C-1440, C-1441, C-1442, C-1449, C-1527, C-1528, C-1529, …+2
- `ARCKEY`: C-881, C-1806, C-1817, C-1820, C-1821, C-1822, C-1841
- `attachAdditionallnformation`: C-572, C-610, C-1074, C-1140, C-1141, C-1142, C-1143, C-1144, C-1557, C-1558, C-1559, C-1560, …+11
- `AttachmentSize`: C-672, C-727, C-864, C-923
